${item.name}
R${item.price.toFixed(2)} each
${item.qty}
R${(item.price*item.qty).toFixed(2)}
`).join(''); const total = cart.reduce((s,i) => s+i.price*i.qty, 0); document.getElementById('cartTotal').textContent = `R${total.toFixed(2)}`; foot.style.display = 'block'; } function changeQty(id, delta) { const item = cart.find(c => c.id === id); if (!item) return; item.qty += delta; if (item.qty <= 0) cart = cart.filter(c => c.id !== id); else if (item.qty > item.maxQty) { item.qty = item.maxQty; showToast('Max stock reached'); } saveCart(); updateCartCount(); renderCart(); } function removeFromCart(id) { cart = cart.filter(c => c.id !== id); saveCart(); updateCartCount(); renderCart(); } // ── WhatsApp order ──────────────────────────────────────────── function sendWhatsApp() { const name = document.getElementById('custName').value.trim(); const phone = document.getElementById('custPhone').value.trim(); const address = document.getElementById('custAddress').value.trim(); if (!name) { showToast('Please enter your name'); return; } if (!phone) { showToast('Please enter your phone number'); return; } if (!cart.length) { showToast('Your cart is empty'); return; } const total = cart.reduce((s,i) => s+i.price*i.qty, 0); const lines = cart.map(i => ` • ${i.name} x${i.qty} = R${(i.price*i.qty).toFixed(2)}`).join('\n'); const msg = encodeURIComponent( `🛍 *New ${storeName} Order*\n\n` + `*Customer:* ${name}\n*Phone:* ${phone}\n` + (address ? `*Address:* ${address}\n` : '') + `\n*Items:*\n${lines}\n\n*Total: R${total.toFixed(2)}*\n\nPlease confirm this order. Thank you! 💛` ); window.open(`https://wa.me/${waNumber}?text=${msg}`, '_blank'); } // ── Drawers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── function closeAll() { document.getElementById('cartDrawer').classList.remove('open'); document.getElementById('overlay').classList.remove('open'); } function openAdmin() { document.getElementById('adminDrawer').classList.add('open'); document.getElementById('overlay').classList.add('open'); if (adminUnlocked) renderAdminProducts(); document.getElementById('waNumber').value = waNumber; document.getElementById('sStoreName').value = storeName; document.getElementById('sTagline').value = storeTagline; document.getElementById('sHeroTitle').value = heroTitleVal.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,''); document.getElementById('sHeroSub').value = heroSubVal; } function closeAdmin() { document.getElementById('adminDrawer').classList.remove('open'); document.getElementById('overlay').classList.remove('open'); } // ── Admin auth ──────────────────────────────────────────────── function checkPwd() { if (document.getElementById('adminPwd').value === adminPwd) { adminUnlocked = true; document.getElementById('pwdGate').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('adminContent').style.display = 'block'; renderAdminProducts(); } else { showToast('Incorrect password'); document.getElementById('adminPwd').value = ''; } } function changePassword() { const np = document.getElementById('newPwd').value.trim(); if (!np || np.length < 4) { showToast('Password must be at least 4 characters'); return; } adminPwd = np; store.set('lh_pwd', adminPwd); document.getElementById('newPwd').value = ''; showToast('Password updated ✓'); } // ── Admin products ──────────────────────────────────────────── function toggleSale() { document.getElementById('saleField').style.display = document.getElementById('pOnSale').checked ? 'block' : 'none'; } function saveProduct() { const name = document.getElementById('pName').value.trim(); const price = parseFloat(document.getElementById('pPrice').value); const stock = parseInt(document.getElementById('pStock').value); if (!name) { showToast('Product name required'); return; } if (isNaN(price)||price<0) { showToast('Enter a valid price'); return; } if (isNaN(stock)||stock<0) { showToast('Enter a valid stock count'); return; } const onSale = document.getElementById('pOnSale').checked; const discount = onSale ? parseInt(document.getElementById('pDiscount').value)||0 : 0; const editId = document.getElementById('editId').value; const data = { id: editId || crypto.randomUUID(), name, price, stock, category: document.getElementById('pCategory').value, texture: document.getElementById('pTexture').value, length: document.getElementById('pLength').value.trim(), color: document.getElementById('pColor').value.trim(), image: document.getElementById('pImage').value.trim(), desc: document.getElementById('pDesc').value.trim(), onSale, discount }; if (editId) { const i = products.findIndex(p=>p.id===editId); if(i!==-1) products[i]=data; } else products.push(data); saveProducts(); clearForm(); renderProducts(); renderAdminProducts(); showToast(editId ? 'Product updated ✓' : 'Product added ✓'); } function editProduct(id) { const p = products.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!p) return; document.getElementById('editId').value = p.id; document.getElementById('pName').value = p.name; document.getElementById('pPrice').value = p.price; document.getElementById('pStock').value = p.stock; document.getElementById('pCategory').value = p.category; document.getElementById('pTexture').value = p.texture||''; document.getElementById('pLength').value = p.length||''; document.getElementById('pColor').value = p.color||''; document.getElementById('pDesc').value = p.desc||''; document.getElementById('pOnSale').checked = p.onSale||false; document.getElementById('pDiscount').value = p.discount||''; document.getElementById('saleField').style.display = p.onSale?'block':'none'; document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Edit Product'; document.getElementById('cancelEdit').style.display = 'block'; if (p.image) { document.getElementById('pImage').value = p.image; document.getElementById('imagePreview').src = p.image; document.getElementById('imagePreview').style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('dropPlaceholder').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('clearImageBtn').style.display = 'block'; } document.querySelector('.admin-body').scrollTop = 0; } function cancelEdit() { clearForm(); } function clearForm() { ['pName','pPrice','pStock','pLength','pColor','pDesc','pDiscount','editId'] .forEach(id => document.getElementById(id).value = ''); document.getElementById('pCategory').selectedIndex = 0; document.getElementById('pTexture').selectedIndex = 0; document.getElementById('pOnSale').checked = false; document.getElementById('saleField').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('cancelEdit').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('formTitle').textContent = 'Add New Product'; clearImage({stopPropagation:()=>{}}); } function deleteProduct(id) { if (!confirm('Delete this product?')) return; products = products.filter(p=>p.id!==id); cart = cart.filter(c=>c.id!==id); saveProducts(); saveCart(); updateCartCount(); renderProducts(); renderAdminProducts(); showToast('Product deleted'); } function renderAdminProducts() { document.getElementById('productCount').textContent = products.length; const list = document.getElementById('adminProductList'); if (!products.length) { list.innerHTML = `

No products yet.

`; return; } list.innerHTML = products.map(p => `
${p.name}${p.category} · R${p.price.toFixed(2)} · Stock: ${p.stock}
`).join(''); } // ── WhatsApp number ─────────────────────────────────────────── function saveWaNumber() { const n = document.getElementById('waNumber').value.trim().replace(/\D/g,''); if (!n) { showToast('Enter a valid number'); return; } waNumber = n; store.set('lh_wa', n); showToast('WhatsApp number saved ✓'); } // ── Image drag & drop ───────────────────────────────────────── function handleDragOver(e) { e.preventDefault(); document.getElementById('dropZone').classList.add('dragover'); } function handleDragLeave() { document.getElementById('dropZone').classList.remove('dragover'); } function handleDrop(e) { e.preventDefault(); document.getElementById('dropZone').classList.remove('dragover'); const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0]; if (file && file.type.startsWith('image/')) loadImageFile(file); else showToast('Please drop an image file'); } function handleFileSelect(e) { const f = e.target.files[0]; if(f) loadImageFile(f); } function loadImageFile(file) { if (file.size > 5*1024*1024) showToast('Large image — resizing…'); const reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = e => { const img = new Image(); img.onload = () => { const canvas = document.createElement('canvas'); const MAX = 800; let w=img.width, h=img.height; if (w>MAX||h>MAX) { if(w>h){h=Math.round(h*MAX/w);w=MAX;}else{w=Math.round(w*MAX/h);h=MAX;} } canvas.width=w; canvas.height=h; canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img,0,0,w,h); const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.82); document.getElementById('pImage').value = dataUrl; document.getElementById('imagePreview').src = dataUrl; document.getElementById('imagePreview').style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('dropPlaceholder').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('clearImageBtn').style.display = 'block'; }; img.src = e.target.result; }; reader.readAsDataURL(file); } function clearImage(e) { e.stopPropagation(); document.getElementById('pImage').value = ''; document.getElementById('imagePreview').src = ''; document.getElementById('imagePreview').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('dropPlaceholder').style.display = 'block'; document.getElementById('clearImageBtn').style.display = 'none'; document.getElementById('fileInput').value = ''; } // ── Init ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── applyBranding(); updateCartCount(); renderProducts();
